Structural cue: the lands that house power become the medium of فساد in 89:12. Assigned role: `البلاد` are social containers whose internal balance has failed. Abductive move: I infer that overstepping destabilizes the very dwelling-spaces it uses. Reading change: the ayah reads as civic breakdown rather than merely imperial expansion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001","root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_91e831ff01a1798490ff","text":"1. **Transgression as hydraulic overflow through lands.** Lexical evidence: `ط غ ي B001` overstepping the bound in rebellion; `ط غ ي B002` overflowing and overwhelming force; `ب ل د B001` bounded tract or place of land. Structural cue: the phrase places overstepping “in” bounded places after valley, pillars, and pegs. Assigned role: transgression is not a private defect but an overflow that crosses spatial and political boundaries. Abductive move: I infer the hydraulic model from the earlier valley-channel (`و د ي B005`) and later pouring (`ص ب ب B001`). Reading change: the ayah reads as social flood, not just moral excess.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001","root_000937/B001","root_000937/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_99d9f9c76b7150968a10","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:11:2\",\"root_000937\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"89:11:4\",\"root_000148\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Azgınlık, sınırlı ülkelerin sınırlarını aşarak yayılan ve toplumsal alanı basan bir taşkınlık gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000148/B001","root_000937/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c7400bf5fb3332215695","text":"1. **Social overstepping figured as overflow across bounded tracts.** **Evidence/cue:** `ط غ ي B001` is overstepping a proper limit and `ط غ ي B002` overflowing with overwhelming force; `ب ل د B001` supplies a bounded tract. The next ayah multiplies corruption, and 89:13 pours punishment downward. **Mechanism:** the land supplies the boundary, transgression the force crossing it, multiplication the rising volume, and pouring the counter-flow. The hydraulic causality is inferred from the packet's sequence. **Reading change:** the conduct is systemic overflow spreading through delimited lands, not a set of placeless private offenses.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_cc9598e0239a9cd5b02a","text":"89:12-13 completed the fluid mechanism: increase supplied rising volume and *sabba* supplied the counter-pour, making `ط غ ي B002` more diagnostic than a generic rebellion gloss. 89:15-16 also corrected a purely imperial reading by bringing the same lordship contrast into ordinary judgments about provision.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d4ec7d88faa00dda6171","text":"89:12’s فساد made `البلاد` read as damaged containers: the geography is not decorative but the field whose balance is spoiled.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000937/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fd4271374b419971e6ac","text":"2. **Overstepping embodied in rival authority.** **Evidence/cue:** `ط غ ي B003` names false authority or a deviant leader, while the relative plural gathers Aad, Thamud, and Pharaoh and repeatedly contrasts them with `ر ب ب B001`, lordship and authority. **Mechanism:** the leaders are not merely agents who commit excess; their authority organizes and reproduces it.
